- 42 test(s), one per behaviour; sealed: tests/test_auth_callback.py, tests/test_config.py, tests/test_credentials.py, tests/test_error_log.py, tests/test_imports.py, tests/test_shell_access.py, tests/test_store.py
- a test for every invariant of the contract: I1, I2, I3
- question to the contract Q1: «GET /auth/callback — … перевіряє … що надано всі SCOPES (інакше MissingScopes → назад на вхід)» and «exchange … рахує missing … непорожній — MissingScopes(missing)». Does the callback itself check Tokens.scopes against SCOPES, or does it rely on exchange raising MissingScopes? With a GoogleAuth who — reading taken: Undisputed tests use only exchange raising MissingScopes (both readings answer 302 to /auth/login). The stricter reading — the callback also checks Tokens.scopes — is tested only in the disputed file.
- question to the contract Q2: «account_id порожній, з /, . чи .. — теж ValueError»: does «з .» mean an account_id that IS "." (as «..» and the Broken-by list "a/b", "..", "" suggest) or one that CONTAINS a dot anywhere ("a.b")? A Google sub is digits, so it matters only for what document_path accepts. — reading taken: I2 tests only "." and ".." as whole ids (rejected under both readings). The stricter reading — any id containing a dot is refused — is tested only in the disputed file.
- question to the contract Q3: The callback must check email_verified, but the contract does not say which error or response an unverified email gets (NotAllowed → 403, NotSignedIn → 302 to login, or 400). — reading taken: No status is asserted: the test checks only what every reading shares — no mm_session, no tokens written, not redirected to /, and / does not serve the shell.
- question to the contract Q4: ALLOWED_EMAILS is read from the environment into frozenset[str], but its format is not given: comma-separated? whitespace allowed? case folded (Google may present Mama@Gmail.com)? — reading taken: Tests use one lower-case address in the environment and exact membership; no test of the separator or of case.
- question to the contract Q5: «Усе з Cache-Control: no-cache» (manifest and static) and the smoke checks «Cache-Control: no-cache на / і статиці» — is the 302 that GET / answers without a session also required to carry no-cache, or only the 200 shell? — reading taken: Tests check no-cache only on the 200 shell, the manifest and the icons; nothing on redirects.
- NOT SEALED until its question is answered: tests/test_access_shell_disputed.py (Q1, Q2)
- RED run by the script: every test fails in its body

## 2026-11-15T14:26:16Z — confirm-edit — the tester's hand-in (contract)
- 48 test(s), one per behaviour; sealed: tests/test_confirm_edit.py
- a test for every invariant of the contract: I1, I2
- question to the contract Q1: The move question «Перенести «X» з <вівторок 10 листопада, 14:00> на <четвер 12 листопада, 10:00>?»: are the placeholders filled literally (nominative, lower-case weekday, «<weekday> <d> <month>, <HH:MM>»), or is the text free to inflect («з вівторка …») as long as both days and times are named? — reading taken: Filled literally: «Перенести «Лікар» з вівторок 10 листопада, 14:00 на четвер 12 листопада, 10:00?». The undisputed tests only require the title, both days and both times, old before new.
- question to the contract Q2: An event label is «<день тижня> <д> <місяць>, <ГГ:ХХ>» «(як картка)»; the card writes the day as «Вівторок, 10 листопада» (capital, comma after the weekday). Which wins: the pattern as written or the card's form? — reading taken: The pattern as written: «вівторок 10 листопада, 14:00», «пʼятниця 13 листопада, 09:00». The undisputed tests check only the weekday case-insensitively, the day, the month and the HH:MM time.
- question to the contract Q3: In «Котрий «X»?», is X the title the model passed (e.g. «лікар») or the title of the found events («Лікар»)? — reading taken: The found events' title, as confirm-core's questions name the element's title («Видалити подію «Лікар»» for title «лікар»). Undisputed tests pass the title in the found case.
- question to the contract Q4: The contract writes the weekday with an ASCII apostrophe («п'ятниця»); the project's weekday list uses «пʼятниця» (U+02BC). Does a weekday said with either apostrophe choose? — reading taken: Both choose. The undisputed test says «пʼятниця»; the disputed one says «п'ятниця».
- question to the contract Q5: «час, що збігається рівно з одним»: in what form is the time said? With confirm-core's normalisation dropping punctuation inside a phrase (its Q3, answered «stands»), «14:00» becomes «1400». Must «14:00» as written on the label choose? — reading taken: Yes: «14:00» said under a choose step chooses the event at 14:00.
- question to the contract Q6: «нерозібрана відповідь» under choose: which answer is it, given «інше — None»? Is a weekday or time that matches several (or none) the same question again, counted toward the two-repeat limit, or «інше» → None? — reading taken: A weekday matching two events is an unparsed answer: the same question again, counted; the third such answer ends with «Добре, нічого не змінюю.» and removes the proposal.
- NOT SEALED until its question is answered: tests/test_confirm_edit_disputed.py (Q1, Q2, Q3, Q4, Q5, Q6)
- RED run by the script: every test fails in its body
